class=\"url\"><a rel=\"mw:ExtLink nofollow\" href=\"https://git.cgsecurity.org/cgit/testdisk/\" class=\"external text\">git<wbr>.cgsecurity<wbr>.org<wbr>/cgit<wbr>/testdisk<wbr>/</a></span> <span class=\"mw-valign-text-top noprint\" typeof=\"mw:File/Frameless\" data-mw=\"{&quot;caption&quot;:&quot;Edit this at Wikidata&quot;}\"><a href=\"https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/Q928564#P1324\" data-file-height=\"20\" data-file-type=\"drawing\" height=\"10\" width=\"10\" class=\"mw-file-element\"></a></span></li></ul>\n</div></td></tr></tbody></table><span about=\"#mwt2\"> </span>\n\n<p id=\"mwBA\"><b id=\"mwBQ\">TestDisk</b> is a <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_and_open-source\" title=\"Free and open-source\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwBg\">free and open-source</a> <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Data_recovery\" title=\"Data recovery\" id=\"mwBw\">data recovery</a> utility that helps users recover lost <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Partition_(computing)\" title=\"Partition (computing)\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwCA\">partitions</a> or repair corrupted filesystems.<sup about=\"#mwt8\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-1\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-1&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-1\" id=\"mwCQ\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwCg\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwCw\">[</span>1<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwDA\">]</span></span></a></sup> TestDisk can collect detailed information about a corrupted drive, which can then be sent to a technician for further analysis. TestDisk supports <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MS-DOS\" title=\"MS-DOS\" id=\"mwDQ\">MS-DOS</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Windows\" title=\"Windows\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwDg\">Windows</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Linux\" title=\"Linux\" id=\"mwDw\">Linux</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FreeBSD\" title=\"FreeBSD\" id=\"mwEA\">FreeBSD</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NetBSD\" title=\"NetBSD\" id=\"mwEQ\">NetBSD</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/OpenBSD\" title=\"OpenBSD\" id=\"mwEg\">OpenBSD</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SunOS\" title=\"SunOS\" id=\"mwEw\">SunOS</a>, and <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MacOS\" title=\"MacOS\" id=\"mwFA\">MacOS</a>. TestDisk handles non-partitioned and partitioned media.<sup about=\"#mwt11\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-grenier2021_2-0\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;grenier2021&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-grenier2021-2&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-grenier2021-2\" id=\"mwFQ\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwFg\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwFw\">[</span>2<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwGA\">]</span></span></a></sup> In particular, it recognizes the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GUID_Partition_Table\" title=\"GUID Partition Table\" id=\"mwGQ\">GUID Partition Table</a> (GPT), <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apple_partition_map\" title=\"Apple partition map\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwGg\">Apple partition map</a>, PC/Intel BIOS partition tables, Sun <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Solaris_(operating_system)\" title=\"Solaris (operating system)\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwGw\">Solaris</a> <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Slice_(disk)\" title=\"Slice (disk)\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwHA\">slice</a> and <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Xbox_(console)\" title=\"Xbox (console)\" id=\"mwHQ\">Xbox</a> fixed partitioning scheme. TestDisk uses a <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Command-line_interface\" title=\"Command-line interface\" id=\"mwHg\">command line user interface</a>. In one test TestDisk recovered deleted files with 97% accuracy.<sup about=\"#mwt14\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-:0_3-0\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;:0&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-:0-3&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-:0-3\" id=\"mwHw\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwIA\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwIQ\">[</span>3<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwIg\">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about=\"#mwt17\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-4\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-4&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-4\" id=\"mwIw\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwJA\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwJQ\">[</span>4<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwJg\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n<meta property=\"mw:PageProp/toc\" id=\"mwJw\" data-mw=\"{&quot;autoGenerated&quot;:true}\"></section><section data-mw-section-id=\"1\" id=\"mwKA\" aria-labelledby=\"Features\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Features\">Features</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=TestDisk&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1\" title=\"Edit section: Features\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwKQ\">TestDisk can recover deleted partitions, rebuild partition tables or rewrite the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Master_boot_record\" title=\"Master boot record\" id=\"mwKg\">master boot record</a> (MBR).<sup about=\"#mwt20\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-5\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-5&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-5\" id=\"mwKw\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwLA\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwLQ\">[</span>5<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwLg\">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about=\"#mwt21\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-:0_3-1\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;:0&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-:0-3\" id=\"mwLw\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwMA\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwMQ\">[</span>3<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwMg\">]</span></span></a></sup> EFI <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GUID_Partition_Table\" title=\"GUID Partition Table\" id=\"mwMw\">GPT</a> is also supported.<sup about=\"#mwt24\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-6\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-6&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-6\" id=\"mwNA\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwNQ\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwNg\">[</span>6<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwNw\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n<section data-mw-section-id=\"2\" id=\"mwOA\" aria-labelledby=\"Partition_recovery\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading3\"><h3 id=\"Partition_recovery\">Partition recovery</h3><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=TestDisk&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2\" title=\"Edit section: Partition recovery\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwOQ\">TestDisk retrieves the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Logical_block_addressing\" title=\"Logical block addressing\" id=\"mwOg\">LBA</a> size and <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cylinder-head-sector\" title=\"Cylinder-head-sector\" id=\"mwOw\">CHS</a> geometry of attached <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Data_storage_device\" title=\"Data storage device\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwPA\">data storage devices</a> (i.e. <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hard_disk\" title=\"Hard disk\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwPQ\">hard disks</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Memory_card\" title=\"Memory card\" id=\"mwPg\">memory cards</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/USB_flash_drive\" title=\"USB flash drive\" id=\"mwPw\">USB flash drives</a>, and <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virtual_disk\" title=\"Virtual disk\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwQA\">virtual disk</a> images) from the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BIOS\" title=\"BIOS\" id=\"mwQQ\">BIOS</a> or the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Operating_system\" title=\"Operating system\" id=\"mwQg\">operating system</a>. The geometry information is required for a successful recovery. TestDisk reads sectors on the storage device to determine if the partition table or filesystem on it requires repair (see next section).</p>\n\n<p id=\"mwQw\">TestDisk is able to recognize the following partition table formats:<sup about=\"#mwt25\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-grenier2021_2-1\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;grenier2021&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-grenier2021-2\" id=\"mwRA\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwRQ\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwRg\">[</span>2<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwRw\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n<ul id=\"mwSA\"><li id=\"mwSQ\">Apple partition map</li>\n<li id=\"mwSg\">GUID Partition Table</li>\n<li id=\"mwSw\">Humax</li>\n<li id=\"mwTA\">PC/Intel Partition Table (master boot record)</li>\n<li id=\"mwTQ\">Sun Solaris slice</li>\n<li id=\"mwTg\">Xbox fixed partitioning scheme</li>\n<li id=\"mwTw\">Non-partitioned media</li></ul>\n\n<p id=\"mwUA\">TestDisk can perform deeper checks to locate partitions that have been deleted from the partition table.<sup about=\"#mwt26\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-grenier2021_2-2\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;grenier2021&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-grenier2021-2\" id=\"mwUQ\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwUg\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwUw\">[</span>2<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwVA\">]</span></span></a></sup> However, it is up to the user to look over the list of possible partitions found by TestDisk and to select those that they wish to recover.</p>\n\n<p id=\"mwVQ\">After partitions are located, TestDisk can rebuild the partition table and rewrite the MBR.<sup about=\"#mwt27\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-grenier2021_2-3\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;grenier2021&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-grenier2021-2\" id=\"mwVg\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwVw\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwWA\">[</span>2<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwWQ\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"3\" id=\"mwWg\" aria-labelledby=\"Filesystem_repair\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading3\"><h3 id=\"Filesystem_repair\">Filesystem repair</h3><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=TestDisk&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3\" title=\"Edit section: Filesystem repair\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwWw\">TestDisk can deal with some specific logical filesystem corruption.<sup about=\"#mwt30\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-7\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-7&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-7\" id=\"mwXA\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwXQ\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwXg\">[</span>7<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwXw\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"4\" id=\"mwYA\" aria-labelledby=\"File_recovery\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading3\"><h3 id=\"File_recovery\">File recovery</h3><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=TestDisk&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4\" title=\"Edit section: File recovery\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwYQ\">When a file is deleted, the list of disk clusters occupied by the file is erased, marking those sectors available for use by other files created or modified thereafter. TestDisk can recover deleted files especially if the file was not fragmented and the clusters have not been reused.</p>\n\n<p id=\"mwYg\">There are two file recovery mechanisms in the TestDisk package:<sup about=\"#mwt31\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-grenier2021_2-4\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;grenier2021&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-grenier2021-2\" id=\"mwYw\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwZA\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwZQ\">[</span>2<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwZg\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n<ul id=\"mwZw\"><li id=\"mwaA\">TestDisk proper uses knowledge of the filesystem structure to perform \"undelete\".</li>\n<li id=\"mwaQ\"><a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PhotoRec\" title=\"PhotoRec\" id=\"mwag\">PhotoRec</a> is a \"file carver\". It does not need any knowledge of the file system, but instead looks for patterns of known file formats in the partition or disk image. It works best on unfragmented files and cannot recover the file name.</li></ul>\n\n</section></section><section data-mw-section-id=\"5\" id=\"mwaw\" aria-labelledby=\"Digital_forensics\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Digital_forensics\">Digital forensics</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=TestDisk&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5\" title=\"Edit section: Digital forensics\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwbA\">TestDisk can be used in <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Digital_forensics\" title=\"Digital forensics\" id=\"mwbQ\">digital forensics</a> to retrieve partitions that were deleted long ago.<sup about=\"#mwt32\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-:0_3-2\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;:0&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-:0-3\" id=\"mwbg\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwbw\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwcA\">[</span>3<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwcQ\">]</span></span></a></sup> It can mount various types of disk images including the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/EnCase#Expert_Witness_File_Format\" title=\"EnCase\" id=\"mwcg\">Expert Witness File Format</a> used by <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/EnCase\" title=\"EnCase\" id=\"mwcw\">EnCase</a>.<sup about=\"#mwt33\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-grenier2021_2-5\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;grenier2021&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-grenier2021-2\" id=\"mwdA\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwdQ\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwdg\">[</span>2<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwdw\">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about=\"#mwt34\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-altheide2011_8-0\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;altheide2011&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-altheide2011-8&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-altheide2011-8\" id=\"mweA\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mweQ\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mweg\">[</span>8<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwew\">]</span></span></a></sup> Binary <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Disk_image\" title=\"Disk image\" id=\"mwfA\">disk images</a>, such as those created with <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ddrescue\" title=\"Ddrescue\" id=\"mwfQ\">ddrescue</a>, can be read by TestDisk as though they were storage devices.<sup about=\"#mwt37\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-nemeth2015_9-0\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;nemeth2015&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-nemeth2015-9&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-nemeth2015-9\" id=\"mwfg\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwfw\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwgA\">[</span>9<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwgQ\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n<p id=\"mwgg\">In TestDisk versions prior to version 7, a malformed disk or its image can be used to inject malicious code into a running TestDisk application on <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cygwin\" title=\"Cygwin\" id=\"mwgw\">Cygwin</a>.<sup about=\"#mwt38\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-nemeth2015_9-1\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;nemeth2015&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-nemeth2015-9\" id=\"mwhA\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwhQ\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwhg\">[</span>9<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwhw\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"6\" id=\"mwiA\" aria-labelledby=\"File_system_support\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"File_system_support\">File system support</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=TestDisk&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6\" title=\"Edit section: File system support\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwiQ\">File system support for TestDisk is shown in the
